1: # Block Puzzle Editor (排面编辑器 / 视频素材生成器)
2: 
3: 这是一个基于 **PIXI.js** 和 **GSAP** 构建的网页端高级方块游戏排面编辑器。
4: 它的核心目标不仅仅是一个可玩的拼图游戏，而是作为一款**专业的视频素材生成工具**，用于为主播、短视频创作者快速构建特定的游戏残局（排面），并录制消除动效作为素材。
5: 
6: ## ✨ 核心特性
7: 
8: - **🎨 自由绘制与智能生成**
9:   - 使用鼠标在网格上自由绘制目标形状，引擎会自动将你画的“洞”智能拆分为 `1x1` 到 `1x4` 的独立方块。
10:   - **物理合法性校验**：采用回溯算法确保所有生成的方块都拥有底部支撑，百分百按照玩家所画的排面生成，不会出现悬空和意外掉落。
11: - **⚙️ 高级配置与控制面板**
12:   - 支持调整网格尺寸、列数、视口高度、总行数等核心参数。
13:   - 可以拖拽滑块精细调整不同长度方块（1~4格）的生成权重概率。
14:   - 自定义初始关卡（LEVEL）和初始分数（SCORE）。
15: - **🎬 专业级视频素材录制**
16:   - 内置基于 `MediaRecorder` 的一键视频录制系统（WebM 格式）。
17:   - **支持绿幕录制 (Chroma Key)**：为解决浏览器原生透明视频录制的底层残影 Bug，专门设计了纯色绿幕录制模式，方便后期在 Premiere Pro 或 After Effects 中一键抠图叠加。
18:   - 录制的视频画面无缝融合了上方的分数 UI 面板与下方的游戏动态画布。
19: - **⚡ 流畅的交互与动效**
20:   - 使用 GSAP 打造平滑的方块选中、缩小以及消除退出动效。
21:   - 支持画面的持续向上滚动（模拟视角下移），碰到顶部边界触发 Game Over。
22: - **💾 存档管理系统**
23:   - 支持多档位的存盘与读取，数据自动持久化到本地 `localStorage`，随时随地恢复你精心设计的残局。
24: 
25: ## 🛠️ 技术栈
26: 
27: - **构建工具**: [Vite](https://vitejs.dev/)
28: - **语言**: [TypeScript](https://www.typescriptlang.org/)
29: - **渲染引擎**: [PIXI.js](https://pixijs.com/) (v8)
30: - **动画引擎**: [GSAP](https://greensock.com/gsap/)
31: - **UI & 样式**: HTML5 + Vanilla CSS
32: 
33: ## 🚀 本地开发与运行
34: 
35: 1. **安装依赖**
36:    请确保你已经安装了 `Node.js` 和 `npm`。在项目根目录下运行：
37:    ```bash
38:    npm install
39:    ```
40: 
41: 2. **启动开发服务器**
42:    ```bash
43:    npm run dev
44:    ```
45:    启动后，浏览器会自动打开或你可以访问终端中输出的 `localhost` 地址。
46: 
47: 3. **构建生产版本**
48:    ```bash
49:    npm run build
50:    ```
51:    构建后的文件将输出到 `dist` 目录。
52: 
53: ## 🎮 使用指南
54: 
55: 1. **设置排面参数**: 页面右侧面板可以调整镜头行数、方块掉落速度、各尺寸方块的生成概率。
56: 2. **绘制残局**: 鼠标在中间黑色网格区域点击拖拽，画出你想要的任何方块形状。
57: 3. **生成实心块**: 绘制完成后，点击面板底部的**“生成方块”**按钮，系统会智能将其填充为真实的物理小方块。
58: 4. **开始录制素材**: 
59:    - 如需视频后期抠像，请先勾选右侧的**“录制绿幕”**。
60:    - 点击左侧悬浮菜单的**“开始录制”**。
61:    - 点击**“开始游戏”**让排面向上滚动。
62:    - 鼠标点击画面中想消除的方块即可触发爆炸与积分上涨特效。
63: 5. **保存视频**: 点击**“停止录制”**后，浏览器会自动下载一段 `.webm` 格式的高清无损视频素材。
64: 
The above content shows the entire, complete file contents of the requested file.

1: import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
2: import fs from 'fs';
3: import path from 'path';
4: import url from 'url';
5: import ffmpeg from 'fluent-ffmpeg';
6: import ffmpegInstaller from '@ffmpeg-installer/ffmpeg';
7: 
8: ffmpeg.setFfmpegPath(ffmpegInstaller.path);
9: 
10: const progressMap = new Map<string, number>();
11: 
12: export default defineConfig({
13:   base: './',
14:   build: {
15:     outDir: process.env.VERCEL ? 'dist' : '../个人Blog/public/playables/block-puzzle',
16:     emptyOutDir: true,
17:   },
18:   plugins: [
19:     {
20:       name: 'ffmpeg-converter',
21:       configureServer(server) {
22:         server.middlewares.use((req, res, next) => {
23:           const parsedUrl = url.parse(req.url!, true);
24:           
25:           if (parsedUrl.pathname === '/api/progress' && req.method === 'GET') {
26:               const taskId = parsedUrl.query.taskId as string;
27:               const pct = progressMap.get(taskId) || 0;
28:               res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json');
29:               res.end(JSON.stringify({ progress: pct }));
30:               return;
31:           }
32: 
33:           if (parsedUrl.pathname === '/api/convert' && req.method === 'POST') {
34:              const taskId = (parsedUrl.query.taskId as string) || Date.now().toString();
35:              const duration = parseFloat((parsedUrl.query.duration as string) || '0');
36:              const mode = parsedUrl.query.mode === 'mp4' ? 'mp4' : 'alpha';
37:              const fpsParam = Number((parsedUrl.query.fps as string) || 30);
38:              const fps = Math.max(24, Math.min(60, Number.isFinite(fpsParam) ? Math.round(fpsParam) : 30));
39:              const webmPath = path.resolve(`./temp_${taskId}.webm`);
40:              const movPath = path.resolve(`./temp_${taskId}.mov`);
41:              const mp4Path = path.resolve(`./temp_${taskId}.mp4`);
42:              
43:              progressMap.set(taskId, 0);
44: 
45:              const writeStream = fs.createWriteStream(webmPath);
46:              req.pipe(writeStream);
47:              
48:              req.on('end', () => {
49:                  if (mode === 'mp4') {
50:                    ffmpeg(webmPath)
51:                     .outputOptions([
52:                         '-map', '0:v:0',
53:                         '-map', '0:a?',
54:                         '-vf', `fps=${fps},format=yuv420p`,
55:                         '-c:v', 'libx264',
56:                         '-preset', 'veryfast',
57:                         '-crf', '20',
58:                         '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p',
59:                         '-threads', '0',
60:                         '-c:a', 'aac',
61:                         '-b:a', '192k',
62:                         '-movflags', '+faststart'
63:                     ])
64:                    .on('progress', (progress) => {
65:                        if (duration > 0 && progress.timemark) {
66:                            const parts = progress.timemark.split(':');
67:                            const h = parseFloat(parts[0]);
68:                            const m = parseFloat(parts[1]);
69:                            const s = parseFloat(parts[2]);
70:                            const totalSeconds = h * 3600 + m * 60 + s;
71:                            let percent = (totalSeconds / duration) * 100;
72:                            if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
73:                            progressMap.set(taskId, percent);
74:                        }
75:                    })
76:                    .save(mp4Path)
77:                    .on('end', () => {
78:                        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'video/mp4');
79:                        res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="direct-output.mp4"');
80:                        const readStream = fs.createReadStream(mp4Path);
81:                        readStream.pipe(res);
82:                        readStream.on('end', () => {
83:                            progressMap.delete(taskId);
84:                            if (fs.existsSync(webmPath)) fs.unlinkSync(webmPath);
85:                            if (fs.existsSync(mp4Path)) fs.unlinkSync(mp4Path);
86:                        });
87:                    })
88:                    .on('error', (err) => {
89:                        console.error('FFmpeg MP4 Conversion Error:', err);
90:                        progressMap.delete(taskId);
91:                        res.statusCode = 500;
92:                        res.end('Conversion failed');
93:                        if (fs.existsSync(webmPath)) fs.unlinkSync(webmPath);
94:                        if (fs.existsSync(mp4Path)) fs.unlinkSync(mp4Path);
95:                    });
96:                    return;
97:                  }
98: 
99:                  ffmpeg(webmPath)
100:                    .complexFilter([
101:                        '[0:v]format=yuv420p,split=2[left][right]', // 强制剥离Chrome录制的残留假Alpha通道！
102:                        '[left]crop=iw/2:ih:0:0[rgb]',       // 左半边：RGB画面
103:                        '[right]crop=iw/2:ih:iw/2:0,format=gray[alpha]',  // 右半边：转为单通道灰度图
104:                        '[rgb][alpha]alphamerge[out]'       // 将灰度图的亮度映射为真正的 Alpha 通道
105:                    ])
106:                     .outputOptions([
107:                         '-map', '[out]',
108:                         '-map', '0:a?',
109:                         '-c:v', 'prores_ks',      // 使用 Apple ProRes 编码器，而不是巨大的 qtrle
110:                         '-profile:v', '4',        // 4 代表 ProRes 4444，支持透明通道且体积大幅减小
111:                         '-pix_fmt', 'yuva444p10le', // 必须指定 10-bit YUV + Alpha 格式才能包含透明通道并被剪辑软件识别
112:                         '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le'       // 标准无损PCM音频，广泛兼容剪辑软件
113:                     ])
114:                    .on('progress', (progress) => {
115:                        if (duration > 0 && progress.timemark) {
116:                            const parts = progress.timemark.split(':');
117:                            const h = parseFloat(parts[0]);
118:                            const m = parseFloat(parts[1]);
119:                            const s = parseFloat(parts[2]);
120:                            const totalSeconds = h * 3600 + m * 60 + s;
121:                            let percent = (totalSeconds / duration) * 100;
122:                            if (percent > 100) percent = 100;
123:                            progressMap.set(taskId, percent);
124:                        }
125:                    })
126:                    .save(movPath)
127:                    .on('end', () => {
128:                        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'video/quicktime');
129:                        res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', 'attachment; filename="combo-material.mov"');
130:                        const readStream = fs.createReadStream(movPath);
131:                        readStream.pipe(res);

134:                            if (fs.existsSync(webmPath)) fs.unlinkSync(webmPath);
135:                            if (fs.existsSync(movPath)) fs.unlinkSync(movPath);
136:                        });
137:                    })
138:                    .on('error', (err) => {
139:                        console.error('FFmpeg Conversion Error:', err);
140:                        progressMap.delete(taskId);
141:                        res.statusCode = 500;
142:                        res.end('Conversion failed');
143:                        if (fs.existsSync(webmPath)) fs.unlinkSync(webmPath);
144:                    });
145:              });
146:              return;
147:           }
148:           next();
149:         });
150:       }
151:     }
152:   ]
153: });

24: export function getPropOccupiedColumns(prop: PropGeometry): number[] {
25:   if (!isValidPropLength(prop.length)) return [];
26: 
27:   const columns: number[] = [];
28:   const direction = prop.propDir || 'left';
29:   const firstCandyColumn = direction === 'left' ? prop.col : prop.col + 1;
30:   const candyCount = prop.length - 1;
31: 
32:   for (let offset = 0; offset < candyCount; offset++) {
33:     columns.push(firstCandyColumn + offset);
34:   }
35: 
36:   return columns;
37: }

39: export function damagePropForClearedRows(
40:   prop: PropState,
41:   clearedRows: number[],
42: ): PropDamageResult {
43:   const triggered = clearedRows.some(row => Math.abs(row - prop.row) <= 1);
44:   if (!triggered || !isValidPropLength(prop.length)) {
45:     return {
46:       triggered: false,
47:       col: prop.col,
48:       length: prop.length,
49:       destroyed: !isValidPropLength(prop.length),
50:     };
51:   }
52: 
53:   const direction = prop.propDir || 'left';
54:   const col = direction === 'left' ? prop.col + 1 : prop.col;
55:   const shortenedLength = prop.length - 1;
56:   const destroyed = !isValidPropLength(shortenedLength);
57: 
58:   return {
59:     triggered: true,
60:     col,
61:     length: destroyed ? 0 : shortenedLength,
62:     destroyed,
63:   };
64: }

4092: function parseMaterialTextureName(fileName: string): { color: string; length: number } | null {
4093:   const baseName = fileName.replace(/\.(png|jpe?g|webp)$/i, '');
4094:   const colorPattern = '(red|blue|green|yellow|pink|红色|蓝色|绿色|黄色|粉色)';
4095:   const standardMatch = baseName.match(new RegExp(`^${colorPattern}[\s_-]*([1-4])$`, 'i'));
4096:   const legacyMatch = baseName.match(new RegExp(`^${colorPattern}1[^\dA-Za-z\u4e00-\u9fff]+([1-4])$`, 'i'));
4097:   const match = standardMatch || legacyMatch;
4098:   if (!match) return null;
4099:   const rawColor = match[1].toLowerCase();
4100:   const color = MATERIAL_COLOR_NAMES[rawColor] || rawColor;
4101:   const length = parseInt(match[2], 10);
4102:   if (isNaN(length) || length < 1 || length > 4) return null;
4103:   return { color, length };
4104: }
4105:

4119: 
4120:       files.push(...await collectMaterialFiles(entry as FileSystemDirectoryHandle));

4123: 
4124:   }
4125: 
4126:   return files;
4127:

8974: 
8975:   const grid = Array.from({ length: PARAMS.totalRows }, () => Array(PARAMS.gridCols).fill(0));
8976: 
8977:   // Holes count as occupied so irregular rows can be "full"
8978: 
8979:   // But skip rows that are ENTIRELY holes (they should never be treated as full)
8980: 
8981:   if (holeMask && holeMask.length > 0) {
8982: 
8983:     for (let r = 0; r < PARAMS.totalRows; r++) {
8984: 
8985:       if (!holeMask[r]) continue;
8986: 
8987:       let hasValidCell = false;
8988: 
8989:       for (let c = 0; c < PARAMS.gridCols; c++) {
8990: 
8991:         if (!holeMask[r][c]) { hasValidCell = true; break; }
8992: 
8993:       }
8994: 
8995:       if (!hasValidCell) continue; // Entire row is holes, skip
8996: 
8997:       for (let c = 0; c < PARAMS.gridCols; c++) {
8998: 
8999:         if (holeMask[r][c]) grid[r][c] = 1;
9000:

9003:     }
9004: 
9005:   }
9006:

9008:

9012:

9014: 
9015:         getPropOccupiedColumns(b).forEach(col => {
9016: 
9017:           if (col >= 0 && col < PARAMS.gridCols) grid[b.row][col] = 1;

9036:

18795: function animatePropShrink(
18796:   sprite: PIXI.Sprite,
18797:   dir: 'left' | 'right',
18798:   oldCol: number,
18799:   oldLen: number,
18800:   newCol: number,
18801:   newLen: number,
18802:   onComplete?: () => void
18803: ) {
18804:   if (!sprite || !sprite.parent) {
18805:     if (onComplete) onComplete();
18806:     return;
18807:   }
18808: 
18809:   const cellSz = PARAMS.cellSize || 50;
18810:   const machineW = cellSz;
18811:   const startWw = oldLen * cellSz;
18812:   const baseYy = sprite.y;
18813:   
18814:   const rightEdge = oldCol * cellSz + oldLen * cellSz;
18815:   const leftEdge = oldCol * cellSz;
18816: 
18817:   const shakeDurL = 400;
18818:   const shrinkDurL = 800;
18819:   const fadeDurL = 350;
18820:   const totalDurL = newLen <= 0 ? shakeDurL + shrinkDurL + fadeDurL : shakeDurL + shrinkDurL;
18821:   const startTimeL = performance.now();
18822: 
18823:   // Create temporary sprites
18824:   const machineSprite = new PIXI.Sprite(getPropTexture(1, dir));
18825:   machineSprite.width = machineW;
18826:   machineSprite.height = cellSz;
18827:   machineSprite.y = baseYy;
18828:   machineSprite.x = dir === 'left' ? rightEdge - machineW : leftEdge;
18829: 
18830:   const candySprite = new PIXI.Sprite(sprite.texture);
18831:   candySprite.width = startWw;
18832:   candySprite.height = cellSz;
18833:   candySprite.y = baseYy;
18834:   candySprite.x = dir === 'left' ? rightEdge - startWw : leftEdge;
18835: 
18836:   const mask = new PIXI.Graphics();
18837:   candySprite.mask = mask;
18838: 
18839:   const container = new PIXI.Container();
18840:   container.addChild(candySprite, machineSprite, mask);
18841:   sprite.parent.addChild(container);
18842: 
18843:   sprite.visible = false;
18844: 
18845:   function stepLast(now: number) {
18846:     const el = now - startTimeL;
18847:     
18848:     if (el < shakeDurL + shrinkDurL) {
18849:       const t = el / (shakeDurL + shrinkDurL);
18850:       const ease = t * (2 - t);
18851:       
18852:       const targetWw = Math.max(newLen * cellSz, machineW);
18853:       const curW = startWw + (targetWw - startWw) * ease;
18854:       
18855:       const shakeX = Math.sin(el * 0.04) * 1;
18856:       const shakeY = Math.cos(el * 0.04) * 0.5;
18857: 
18858:       machineSprite.y = baseYy; // Machine head stays perfectly still!
18859:       candySprite.y = baseYy + shakeY;
18860:       machineSprite.x = dir === 'left' ? rightEdge - machineW : leftEdge; // Machine head stays perfectly still!
18861: 
18862:       let candyX = 0;
18863:       mask.clear();
18864:       mask.beginFill(0xffffff);
18865:       if (dir === 'left') {
18866:         candyX = rightEdge - curW + shakeX;
18867:         candySprite.x = candyX;
18868:         mask.drawRect(candyX, baseYy - 20, Math.max(0, curW - machineW), cellSz + 40); 
18869:       } else {
18870:         candyX = leftEdge - (startWw - curW) + shakeX;
18871:         candySprite.x = candyX;
18872:         mask.drawRect(leftEdge + machineW + shakeX, baseYy - 20, Math.max(0, curW - machineW), cellSz + 40);
18873:       }
18874:       mask.endFill();
18875:       
18876:       requestAnimationFrame(stepLast);
18877:     } else if (el < totalDurL && newLen <= 0) {
18878:       candySprite.visible = false;
18879:       machineSprite.y = baseYy;
18880:       machineSprite.x = dir === 'left' ? rightEdge - machineW : leftEdge;
18881:       machineSprite.alpha = 1 - (el - shakeDurL - shrinkDurL) / fadeDurL;
18882:       requestAnimationFrame(stepLast);
18883:     } else {
18884:       container.destroy({ children: true });
18885:       if (newLen > 0) {
18886:         sprite.texture = getPropTexture(newLen, dir);
18887:         sprite.width = newLen * cellSz;
18888:         sprite.x = dir === 'left' ? rightEdge - sprite.width : leftEdge;
18889:         sprite.y = baseYy;
18890:         sprite.visible = true;
18891:       }
18892:       if (onComplete) onComplete();
18893:     }
18894:   }
18895:   requestAnimationFrame(stepLast);
18896: }
18897:

20: export function isValidPropLength(length: number): boolean {
21:   return length > 1;
22: }
23: 
24: export function getPropOccupiedColumns(prop: PropGeometry): number[] {
25:   if (!isValidPropLength(prop.length)) return [];
26: 
27:   const columns: number[] = [];
28:   const direction = prop.propDir || 'left';
29:   const firstCandyColumn = direction === 'left' ? prop.col : prop.col + 1;
30:   const candyCount = prop.length - 1;
31: 
32:   for (let offset = 0; offset < candyCount; offset++) {
33:     columns.push(firstCandyColumn + offset);
34:   }
35: 
36:   return columns;
37: }
38: 
39: export function damagePropForClearedRows(
40:   prop: PropState,
41:   clearedRows: number[],
42: ): PropDamageResult {
43:   const triggered = clearedRows.some(row => Math.abs(row - prop.row) <= 1);
44:   if (!triggered || !isValidPropLength(prop.length)) {
45:     return {
46:       triggered: false,
47:       col: prop.col,
48:       length: prop.length,
49:       destroyed: !isValidPropLength(prop.length),
50:     };
51:   }
52: 
53:   const direction = prop.propDir || 'left';
54:   const col = direction === 'left' ? prop.col + 1 : prop.col;
55:   const shortenedLength = prop.length - 1;
56:   const destroyed = !isValidPropLength(shortenedLength);
57: 
58:   return {
59:     triggered: true,
60:     col,
61:     length: destroyed ? 0 : shortenedLength,
62:     destroyed,
63:   };
64: }
